Interpolation:
```
def _resample(p, m):
    if m==len(p): return p.copy()
    if len(p)==1: return np.full(m,p[0])
    xp=np.linspace(0,1,len(p)-1)
    fp=np.interp(np.linspace(0,1,m), xp, p)
    # endpoints maybe if p length 1? ok
    s=fp.sum(); if s>0: fp/=s
    else: fp=np.full(m,1/m)
    return np.clip(fp,0,None)
```

Let's define `_c_from_p(p)` using FFT:
```
def _conv_self(a):
    n=a.size
    if n < 512: return np.convolve(a,a) # exact smaller, avoids FFT round?
    size=next_fast_len(2*n-1)
    F=rfft(a,size)
    c=irfft(F*F,size)
    c=c[:2*n-1]
    # Clip small negatives
    c[c < 0] = 0 if small? Use np.maximum(c,0)
    return c
```
For n=384, np.convolve exact maybe faster. For n large, FFT.
But score exact uses np.convolve maybe. For local, FFT round ~1e-15. We clip to 0. The max if negative due round? true max positive. okay.

`_cross_corr(p, w)`:
```
def _cross_corr(p,w):
    n=p.size
    # want q_j = Σ_i p_i w_{j+i}
    # convolve p[::-1] with w; take [n-1: n+n-1] = [n-1:2n-1]? length n
    L=3*n-2
    size=next_fast_len(L)
    pr=p[::-1]
    F=rfft(pr,size)
    G=rfft(w,size)
    q=irfft(F*G,size)[:L]
    q=q[n-1: n+n-1]  # indices n-1 .. 2n-2 inclusive
    np.maximum(q,0,out=q)
    return q
```
Check length n: slice stop n+n-1 =2n-1; if start n-1 length n. For n=2, L=4, start1 stop3 indices1,2. good.
For n=1? length1: L=1, size1, start0 stop1.

Softmax stable:
```
def _softmax(u):
    u = u - np.max(u)
    e=np.exp(u)
    e/=e.sum()
    return e
```
If u has very negative, okay. Initialize u = np.log(p) - mean(logp) maybe for p>0. If p has zeros, set to 1e-300/n. To allow near zero, maybe p_min=1e-300.

Function `_lse_obj(u, T, n=None)`:
- p=softmax(u)
- c=_conv_self(p)
- cmax=np.max(c)
- if T <= 0 exact? Return exact and gradient active? For L-BFGS only T>0.
- scale=(c-cmax)/T
- exps=np.exp(scale)
- S=exps.mean() or sum? Use mean to avoid large; derivative same. If S==0? no.
- f=cmax + T*np.log(S)
- w=exps / exps.sum() (sum=1)
- q=_cross_corr(p,w)
- gdp=2*q
- du=p*(gdp - np.dot(p,gdp))
- return f, du
Need ensure grad shape. If c values all equal? q ~p/n, du maybe small.

Scipy minimize with callback:
```
def _minimize_lbfgs(u, T, stop_time, maxiter=2000, gtol=1e-12, ftol=1e-16):
    stop_flag=[False]
    def cb(xk):
       if time.monotonic() > stop_time - 5:
          stop_flag[0]=True
          return True
       return None
    try:
       res=minimize(_lse_obj, u, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', args=(T,), options={'maxiter':maxiter, 'ftol':ftol, 'gtol':gtol, 'maxls':30, 'maxfun':None}, callback=cb)
       if stop_flag[0] or not res.x size: use u
       else: u=res.x
    except Exception:
       pass
    return u
```

But line search within 120 may exceed time slightly. Acceptable. If callback not working, still okay if maxiter moderate.

Project projection:
```
def _proj_simplex(v):
    n=len(v); u=np.sort(v)[::-1]; css=np.cumsum(u); rho=np.nonzero(u*(np.arange(1,n+1)-1)>css[n-1]? ...)[0][-1]
    theta=(css[rho]-1)/ (rho+1)
    w=np.maximum(v-theta,0)
    # if sum not exactly due tiny, adjust
    return w
```

```
def _coord_polish(p, stop_time, max_sweeps=20, seed=1, n_grid=None):
   n=len(p); p=np.asarray(p,float).copy(); p=np.maximum(p,0)
   S=p.sum(); if S<1e-300: ...
   c=_conv_self(p); m0=c.max()
   best_score=2*n*m0/(S*S)
   # to speed, maybe work with normalized sum1? But formula with S okay.
   # Determine grid size: if n<=512: 48; <=1024:32; <=2048:24; else 16
   max_ids = ...
   for sweep in range(max_sweeps):
      improved=False
      order = np.random.permutation(n) if seed else random
      for i in order:
         if time>stop_time-sec: break
         v=p[i]; S=S # current sum
         start=i; end=min(i+n, 2*n-1); Lslice=end-start
         base=c[start:end]
         infl=2*p[:Lslice]  # p index k-i
         # grid x from 0 to x_max
         x_max = max(20*v, 20/S? Since p sum S, typical 1? If S maybe 1, use 20/S? For sum1, 20/n?
         # Use scale = max(v, 1/n, cmax? )
         # Since p sum maybe 1, individual optimum ~1/n. x_max = max(10*v, 50/n, 10*cmax? cmax*n? no)
         x_max = max(10*v, 50.0/n, 1e-12)
         if x_max < 1e-12: x_max=1e-6
         x_max = min(x_max, 1.0) # if S=1, max 1; if S not 1, maybe cap S? Use min(x_max, max(2*S/n, 0.5*S?))
         G = n_grid
         xs=np.linspace(0, x_max, G+1)
         # ensure include v and maybe 0
         # Compute w=xs-v
         denom = S + (xs-v)
         # if denom small invalid
         valid = denom > 1e-15
         # Linear max: base + w[:,None]*infl[None,:]
         # Memory G*Lslice may large; process chunks of 16.
         best_x=v; best_val=best_score
         max_const = max(c[:start], c[end:]) if start>0 or end<Ltot else 0
         diag_idx = 2*i - start if 0 <= 2*i < end else None # within slice
         for chunk xs:
            w=xs-v
            maff = max(base + w[:,None]*infl[None,:])
            if diag_idx is not None:
                maff = np.maximum(maff, base[diag_idx] + w*infl[diag_idx] + w*w)
            maff = np.maximum(maff, max_const)
            vals = 2*n*maff / denom**2
            choose min valid
         # fine grid around best_x: range [max(0,best_x-dw), min(x_max,best_x+dw)] with 20
         # if improved accept: update p[i], S, c slice, c[2i]+=w^2. But if we update c incrementally, max_const for later changes. Good.
      if not improved: break
   # recompute exact c and score
```

```
import numpy as np, time, math
from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft, next_fast_len
from scipy.optimize import minimize

def _get_parent(): ...

def _conv_self(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    n=a.size
    if n<256:
       c=np.convolve(a,a)
    else:
       size=next_fast_len(2*n-1)
       F=rfft(a,size)
       c=irfft(F*F,size)[:2*n-1]
    c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    return c
```

Softmax and LSE:
```
def _softmax(u):
    u=np.asarray(u,float)
    m=np.max(u)
    e=np.exp(u-m)
    s=e.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite: e=np.full(u.size,u.size**-1); s=1
    return e/s
```

```
   while time.monotonic()<stop-20:
```

Coordinate objective vectorized:
```
def _coord_vals(xs, v, S, c, start, end, infl, diag_idx, max_const):
   w=xs-v; denom=S+w; valid=denom>1e-15
   aff = base[None,:] + w[:,None]*infl[None,:]
   maff=aff.max(axis=1)
   if diag is not None: maff=np.maximum(maff, base[diag]+w*infl[diag]+w*w)
   maff=np.maximum(maff, max_const)
   vals = np.full(xs.size, inf)
   vals[valid]=2*n*maff[valid]/denom[valid]**2
   return vals
```

```
def _score_exact_arr(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,float)
   if a.size==0: return inf
   s=a.sum()
   if s<0.01: return inf
   # Evaluator will clip values; our values finite 0..1, okay
   b=np.convolve(a,a)
   m=b.max()
   return 2*a.size*m/(s*s)
```

`_resample_mid(src, n)`:
```
def _resample_mid(src, n):
    src=np.asarray(src,float).ravel()
    m=src.size
    if m==0: return np.full(n,1.0/n)
    if m==1: return np.array([src[0]/float(n)]*n)
    # normalize? preserve shape; sum will be about old sum*m/n? Actually interp of normalized p (sum1) yields sum ~1? linear interpolation in uniform grid: sum ~ (n/m)*old_sum? Since spacing shrinks, sum changes by factor n/m if values not scaled. Need normalize after.
    xp=(np.arange(m)+0.5)/m
    if n==m: out=src.copy()
    else: out=np.interp((np.arange(n)+0.5)/n, xp, src)
    out=np.maximum(out,0)
    s=out.sum()
    if s<=0: out=np.full(n,1/n)
    else: out/=s
    return out
```
If parent has spikes, interpolation smooths. Good.

`_score_fft(p)` for local:
```
def _score_fft(p,n=None):
    n=len(p); s=p.sum()
    if s<0.01: return inf
    # since scale invariant, can use p as is
    c=_conv_self(p); m=c.max()
    return 2*n*m/(s*s)
```
But for softmax p sum=1, s=1.

```
def _coord_polish(p, stop_time, max_sweeps=200, G=None, seed=12345, consider=None):
    n=len(p)
    p=np.maximum(np.asarray(p,float),0).copy()
    s=p.sum()
    if s<0.01: return p, inf
    p = p / s
    if G is None:
       if n <= 450: G=56
       elif n <= 900: G=44
       elif n <= 1800: G=34
       elif n <= 3600: G=26
       else: G=20
    c=_conv_self(p)
    Ltot=2*n-1
    best_exact_local = 2.0*n*c.max()
    best_local = 2.0*n*c.max() # since S=1
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    flat=0; sweep=0
    while time.monotonic() < stop_time - 3 and sweep < max_sweeps:
       improved_sweep=False
       order = rng.permutation(n)
       for i in range(n)? # use order but for i in order
          if time > stop_time-1: break
          v=p[i]
          S=p.sum()  # maybe close1
          # if S deviates >1.5, normalize now? maybe after sweep only. But S could drift if many updates. Do if S>1.2 or <0.8:
          if S > 1.2 or S < 0.8:
             p *= 1/S
             c *= 1/(S*S)
             S=1.0
          v=p[i]
          start=i; end=min(i+n, Ltot); Ls=end-start
          base=c[start:end]
          infl=2.0*p[:Ls]
          # max_const
          if start>0: left=c[:start].max()
          else: left=0
          if end<Ltot: right=c[end:].max()
          else: right=0
          max_const=max(left,right)
          cmax=c.max()
          scale1=S/n
          x_hi = max(20.0*v, 60.0*scale1, 0.05*S, 1e-12)
          x_hi = min(x_hi, 10.0*S)
          if not finite: x_hi=scale1
          # Make grid
          pts=np.linspace(0.0, x_hi, G+1)
          if 0 < v < x_hi: pts=np.concatenate([pts,[v]])
          pts=np.unique(pts)
          # If x_hi very small, use G smaller
          diag = 2*i - start
          # compute val
          def eval_xs(xs): ... uses base, infl, max_const, diag, S, n
          vals=eval_xs(pts)
          idx=np.argmin(vals); best_x=pts[idx]; best_val=vals[idx]
          # fine
          if G>1:
             dw=x_hi/(G)
             lo=max(0,best_x-2*dw); hi=min(x_hi,best_x+2*dw)
             fine=np.linspace(lo,hi,30)
             if 0<best_x<=x_hi: fine=np.concatenate([fine,[best_x]])
             fine=np.unique(fine)
             vals2=eval_xs(fine)
             idx2=... update
          if best_val < best_local*(1-1e-12) - 1e-15 and best_x not close? and 0<=best_x<=max(x_hi,1e-300):
             w=best_x-v
             if abs(w)>1e-15:
                p[i]=best_x
                S += w
                c[start:end] += w*infl
                if 0 <= 2*i < Ltot: c[2*i] += w*w
                best_local = best_val
                improved_sweep=True
       if time > stop: break
       # normalize
       S=p.sum()
       if S>0:
          p *= 1/S
          c *= 1/(S*S)
       else: break
       c = np.maximum(c,0.0)  # remove negative
       # recompute exact local score maybe
       exact_local = 2*n*c.max()
       if exact_local >= best_local: best_local=exact_local? (c may drift)
       # For local accept? We'll use best_local variable as current exact? Actually after normalize score=exact_local.
       best_current=exact_local
       if improved_sweep:
           # maybe consider exact at end
           if consider: consider(p)
           flat=0
       else:
           flat+=1
           if flat>=5: break
       sweep+=1
    p=np.maximum(p,0); s=p.sum(); if s>0: p/=s
    c=_conv_self(p); sc=2*n*c.max()
    return p, sc
```

```
def _lp_step(p, stop, time_limit=6.0, consider=None):
  n=len(p); M=_conv_self(p).max()
  A=np.zeros((2*n-1,n))
  for j in range(n): A[j:j+n,j]=p
  try:
    res=linprog(-np.ones(n), A_ub=A,b_ub=np.full(2*n-1,M), bounds=[(0,None)]*n, method='highs', options={'presolve':True,'time_limit':time_limit})
  except: return p
  if res.status not in (0,3,4?) and res.x None: return p
  g=np.asarray(res.x); s=g.sum(); if s<=0: return p
  g/=s
  # If g similar to p, skip
  if np.linalg.norm(g-p) <1e-6: return p
  best=p
  for t in (0.2,0.5,0.8):
     q=(1-t)*p+t*g
     q=np.maximum(q,0); q/=q.sum()
     sc=_score_fft(q)
     if sc < _score_fft(p): best=q
  # coordinate mix? Maybe short coord
  return best
```

```
def _cross_corr(p,w):
```

```
def _lbfgs_chunk(u, T, end_time, maxiter=30, gtol=1e-11):
   n=u.size
   best_f, best_u = _lse_value(u,T) maybe. But calling fun gets f,grad.

          break
```

Now, maybe improve by **returning scaled p to use larger coefficients**? Score scale invariant, no. But if p_i extremely small, underflow in evaluator? If some p_i <1e-308, they are 0; okay. Could scale p by e.g. 1.0? sum1. fine.

Let's think if L-BFGS with softmax can get stuck due gauge causing small ‖gradient‖ but not optimal. At optimum under softmax, gradient wrt u has flat direction removed? It should be zero if p satisfies KKT? For sum1 coordinate gradient, stationary condition is gdp = λ*1 on support? Our du = p*(gdp - dot(p,gdp)). For du=0, need gdp_i = dot(p,gdp) for all i with p_i>0. This is p-weighted average, not ordinary affine sum constraint. Wait! This is important. In softmax parameterization, the tangent space at p for variations du corresponds to dp = D p (du), with sum dp = p dot du = 0 (ordinary sum because sum p=1). The first-order condition for constrained minimization over simplex is gradient dp g = λ * 1 on support, i.e., g_i = λ for p_i>0 (ordinary constant), not p-weighted constant. Our gradient wrt u = p_i(g_i - Σ p_j g_j). Setting du=0 gives g_i = Σ p_j g_j for p_i>0, i.e., ordinary constant equal to p-weighted average. Since p_i>0, condition g_i = constant. That's same as ordinary constant with constant = p-weighted average. So correct! Because equality multiplier λ is p-weighted average due parameterization. Good. If some p_i=0, du_i=0 no condition. So softmax gradient okay.

Euclidean projected gradient would use ordinary mean; softmax uses p-weighted mean but resulting dp direction? The stationary condition is same. Step size/gauge differs; fine.

Now, does `du` sum to zero? yes. Softmax step not same as p move; but okay.

Now, if we revert p but not u? We'll set u from p.

Memory in L-BFGS: `scipy.optimize.minimize` stores history; maxcor 20. For n=3072, 20*2*3072*8=1MB, fine.

Potential issue with `minimize` and large n: It may issue `RuntimeWarning` divide by zero; catch? not.

Let's now draft the final code in analysis to ensure syntactic.

```
import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft, next_fast_len
from scipy.optimize import minimize

def _conv_self(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if n <= 512:
        c = np.convolve(a, a, mode='full')
    else:
        L = 2*n - 1
        size = next_fast_len(L)
        F = rfft(a, size)
        c = irfft(F * F, size)[:L]
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    return c

def _cross_corr(p, w):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    n = p.size
    L = 3*n - 2
    size = next_fast_len(L)
    prv = p[::-1].copy()
    F = rfft(prv, size)
    G = rfft(w, size)
    c = irfft(F * G, size)[:L]
    q = c[n-1:2*n-1]
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64)
    np.maximum(q, 0.0, out=q)
    return q
```
For n=1, L=1, q=c[0:1] okay.

`_softmax`.
`_score_fft`:
```
def _score_fft(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return inf
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s<0.01: return inf
    c=_conv_self(a)
    m=float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
    if not math.isfinite(m): return inf
    return 2.0*n*m/(s*s)
```

```
def _lbfgs_optimize(p, n, stop_time, consider=None, fine_only=False):
    p = np.maximum(np.asarray(p,float),0).copy()
    s=p.sum()
    if s < 1e-300: return p
    if abs(s-1)>1e-12: p = p/s
    def softmax... defined global _softmax
    u = np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300)); u -= np.mean(u)
    def lse_func(u):
        p_s = _softmax(u)
        c = _conv_self(p_s)
        cmax = float(c.max())
        if not finite: return 1e100, np.zeros_like(u)
        T = T_holder[0]? If using arg.
```

```
    def run_chunk(u, T, end_stage):
        try: f0,_=lse_func(u,T)
        except: return u
        if not finite: return u
        best_u = u.copy(); best_f=f0
        no_prog=0
        while time.monotonic() < end_stage - 2.0:
            maxiter = 25 if n > 2000 else 40
            opts = {'maxiter': maxiter, 'gtol': 1e-10 if n>1000 else 1e-11, 'ftol':1e-15, 'maxls':20, 'maxcor':20, 'maxdisp':0, 'disp':False}
            try:
                res=minimize(lse_func, best_u, args=(T,), jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', options=opts)
            except Exception:
                break
            if res.x is not None:
                x=np.asarray(res.x,float)
                if x.shape==u.shape and np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
                    f=res.fun
                    if math.isfinite(f) and f < best_f - 1e-15:
                        best_f=f; best_u=x.copy(); no_prog=0
                    elif f < best_f - 1e-12? ... # update even tiny
                    else:
                        no_prog += 1
                        if no_prog >= 2: break
            if res.status == 0:
                break
        return best_u
```

```
def _coord_polish(p, stop_time, max_sweeps=0, G=None, seed=12345, consider=None):
    n = p.size
    p = np.maximum(...).copy()
    s=p.sum(); if s<... return
    p = p/s
    c = _conv_self(p)
    Ltot = 2*n-1
    cur_score = 2.0*n*float(c.max())
    best_p = p.copy(); best_score = cur_score
    if G is None: ...
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    sweep=0; flat=0
    while sweep < max_sweeps and time.monotonic() < stop_time - 2.0:
        improved = False
        order = rng.permutation(n)
        for idx_i in order:
            i=int(idx_i)
            if time.monotonic() > stop_time - 0.5: break
            # normalize if S deviated a lot
            S = float(p.sum())
            if S <= 0: break
            if S > 1.25 or S < 0.75:
                inv=1.0/S; p*=inv; c*=inv*inv; S=1.0
            v = float(p[i])
            start = i; end = min(i+n, Ltot); Ls = end-start
            base = c[start:end]
            # base is view. If we will update after eval, eval uses base values. OK.
            infl = 2.0 * p[:Ls]
            max_const = 0.0
            if start > 0:
                ml = float(c[:start].max()) if start>0 else 0
                if ml > max_const: max_const=ml
            if end < Ltot:
                mr = float(c[end:].max()) if end<Ltot else 0
                if mr > max_const: max_const=mr
            x_hi = max(20.0*v, 60.0*S/n, 0.05*S, 1e-12)
            x_hi = min(x_hi, 10.0*S)
            if not math.isfinite(x_hi) or x_hi <= 0: x_hi = max(1e-8, S/n)
            pts = np.linspace(0.0, x_hi, G+1)
            pts = np.concatenate((pts, [v]))
            pts = np.unique(pts)
            diag = i  # because start=i
            def eval_xs(xs):
                xs = np.asarray(xs, float)
                w = xs - v
                denom = S + w
                vals = np.full(xs.shape, np.inf, dtype=float)
                valid = denom > 1e-15
                if not np.any(valid): return vals
                # restrict valid? For vectorization, compute all but mask
                aff = base[None, :] + w[:, None] * infl[None, :]
                maff = np.max(aff, axis=1)
                dval = base[diag] + w * infl[diag] + w * w
                maff = np.maximum(maff, dval)
                maff = np.maximum(maff, max_const)
                dv = denom[valid]
                mv = maff[valid]
                # if maff invalid for nonvalid? valid false skip
                vals[valid] = 2.0 * n * mv / (dv * dv)
                return vals
            vals = eval_xs(pts)
            # ensure finite
            if not np.any(np.isfinite(vals)):
                continue
            imin = int(np.argmin(vals))
            best_x = float(pts[imin]); best_val = float(vals[imin])
            # fine
            if G > 1:
                dw = x_hi / max(1,G)
                lo = max(0.0, best_x - 2.0*dw)
                hi = min(x_hi, best_x + 2.0*dw)
                if hi <= lo: hi = lo + 1e-12
                fine = np.linspace(lo, hi, 32)
                fine = np.concatenate((fine, [best_x]))
                fine = np.unique(fine)
                vals2 = eval_xs(fine)
                if np.any(np.isfinite(vals2)):
                    imin2=int(np.argmin(vals2)); vx2=float(fine[imin2]); vv2=float(vals2[imin2])
                    if vv2 < best_val: best_x=...; best_val=...
            if best_val < cur_score - 1e-13 and 0 <= best_x <= 10*S:
                w = best_x - v
                if abs(w) > 1e-15:
                    p[i] = best_x
                    S += w
                    c[start:end] += w * infl
                    c[2*i] += w * w
                    cur_score = best_val
                    improved = True
```
But `base = c[start:end]` is a view. In eval, we use base; after `c[start:end]` update, base changed. But eval done before update. Good. However after previous coordinate updates, c is current. Good.

```
def _run_attempt(n, init, end_time, seed, consider):
    p = _resample_mid(init, n)
    consider(p)
    if end_time - time.monotonic() < 20: return p
    phase_len = max(0, end_time - time.monotonic())
    # L-BFGS portion
    lbfgs_stop = min(end_time - 20, time.monotonic() + 0.70*phase_len)
    if lbfgs_stop - time.monotonic() > 15:
        p2 = _lbfgs_optimize(p, n, lbfgs_stop, consider=consider, fine_only=False)
        consider(p2)
    else: p2=p
    # coordinate
    rem = end_time - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 25:
        # coordinate gets up to 45% of remaining or 60s?
        coord_stop = min(end_time - 3, time.monotonic() + max(20, min(120, 0.55*rem)))
        G = None
        p3, _ = _coord_polish(p2, coord_stop, max_sweeps=100000 if False else 1000, G=G, seed=seed, consider=consider)
        consider(p3)
    return p3
```

Now, code for `_run_attempt`:
```
def _run_attempt(n, src_arr, end_time, seed, do_coord=True):
    nonlocal best_arr,best_score
    # If end_time <= now+15 return
    p = _resample_mid(src_arr, n)
    consider(p)
    if best_arr.size == n: p = best_arr.copy() if _score_fft(best_arr) < _score_fft(p) else p
    if end_time - time.monotonic() < 25: return
    phase_len = max(0.0, end_time - time.monotonic())
    # L-BFGS
    lbfgs_frac = 0.72
    lbfgs_stop = min(end_time - 15, time.monotonic() + lbfgs_frac*phase_len)
    if lbfgs_stop - time.monotonic() > 12:
       p2 = _lbfgs_optimize(p, n, lbfgs_stop, consider=consider, fine_only=False)
       if p2 is not None and p2.size==n: p=p2
       # use best of same length
       if best_arr.size==n and _score_fft(best_arr) < _score_fft(p): p=best_arr.copy()
    # coord
    rem = end_time - time.monotonic()
    if do_coord and rem > 20:
       coord_stop = min(end_time - 2, time.monotonic() + max(20, min(150, 0.28*phase_len)))
       p3, _ = _coord_polish(p, coord_stop, seed=seed, consider=consider)
       p=p3
       if best_arr.size==n and _score_fft(best_arr) < _score_fft(p): p=best_arr.copy()
    consider(p)
```
If phase_len small, lbfgs_stop maybe <= now; skip.

```
def _make_skew(src,n,seed):
    p=_resample_mid(src,n)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
    # random smooth skew chosen from a few
    sig = [0.0]*(n)
    # generate low-frequency noise: rng.normal for Fourier? Simpler: q=p*np.exp(0.8*(2*x-1)*choice)
    choice = rng.choice([-1,1]) * (0.4 + 0.4*rng.random())
    q=p*np.exp(choice*(2*x-1))
    q=np.maximum(q,0); s=q.sum(); if s>0 q/=s else q=p
    return q
```
This may introduce asymmetry.

`_make_skew`:
```
def _make_skew(src,n,seed):
   p=_resample_mid(src,n)
   rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
   x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
   side = 1 if rng.random()<0.5 else -1
   amp = 0.5 + 0.8*rng.random()
   q = p * np.exp(side*amp*(2*x-1))
   q=np.maximum(q,0); s=q.sum(); if s>0: q/=s
   else: q=p
   return q
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft, next_fast_len
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def _get_parent():
    try:
        h = globals()["height_sequence_1"]
        p = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        p = np.ones(384, dtype=np.float64)
    if p.size == 0:
        p = np.ones(384, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if float(p.sum()) < 1e-12:
        p = np.ones(384, dtype=np.float64)
    return p


def _conv_self(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if n <= 512:
        c = np.convolve(a, a, mode="full")
    else:
        L = 2 * n - 1
        size = next_fast_len(L)
        F = rfft(a, size)
        c = irfft(F * F, size)[:L]
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    return c


def _cross_corr(p, w):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    n = p.size
    L = 3 * n - 2
    size = next_fast_len(L)
    F = rfft(p[::-1], size)
    G = rfft(w, size)
    c = irfft(F * G, size)[:L]
    q = c[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64)
    np.maximum(q, 0.0, out=q)
    return q


def _softmax(u):
    u = np.asarray(u, dtype=np.float64)
    if u.size == 0:
        return u
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(u)):
        return np.full(u.size, 1.0 / u.size, dtype=np.float64)
    m = float(np.max(u))
    e = np.exp(u - m, dtype=np.float64)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        e = np.full(u.size, 1.0 / u.size, dtype=np.float64)
        s = 1.0
    return e / s


def _score_fft(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return math.inf
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        return math.inf
    c = _conv_self(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return math.inf
    m = float(c.max())
    if not math.isfinite(m) or m < 0.0:
        return math.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * m / (s * s))


def _score_exact_arr(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return math.inf
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        return math.inf
    b = np.convolve(a, a)
    if b.size == 0:
        return math.inf
    m = float(b.max())
    if not math.isfinite(m) or m < 0.0:
        return math.inf
    return float(2.0 * a.size * m / (s * s))


def _resample_mid(src, n):
    src = np.asarray(src, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(max(1, n))
    if src.size == 0:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    if src.size == 1:
        return np.full(n, float(src[0]) / n, dtype=np.float64)
    if src.size == n:
        out = src.copy()
    else:
        m = src.size
        xp = (np.arange(m, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(m)
        fp = np.interp((np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n), xp, src)
        out = fp
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if s < 1e-300 or not math.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    return out / s


def _lbfgs_optimize(p, n, stop_time, consider=None, fine_only=False):
    p = np.maximum(np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel(), 0.0).copy()
    n = int(p.size)
    if n == 0:
        return p
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s < 1e-300 or not math.isfinite(s):
        return p
    p = p / s

    def p_to_u(pp):
        uu = np.log(np.maximum(pp, 1e-300), dtype=np.float64)
        return uu - float(np.mean(uu))

    u = p_to_u(p)

    if n > 0:
        conv_size = next_fast_len(2 * n - 1)
        cross_size = next_fast_len(3 * n - 2)
        conv_len = 2 * n - 1
        cross_len = 3 * n - 2

        def cfast(a):
            F = rfft(a, conv_size)
            c = irfft(F * F, conv_size)[:conv_len]
            np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
            return c

        def xfast(pp, ww):
            F = rfft(pp[::-1], cross_size)
            G = rfft(ww, cross_size)
            c = irfft(F * G, cross_size)[:cross_len]
            q = c[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
            np.maximum(q, 0.0, out=q)
            return q
    else:
        cfast = lambda x: np.zeros(0)
        xfast = lambda x, y: np.zeros(0)

    def lse_func(uu, T):
        pp = _softmax(uu)
        c = cfast(pp)
        if c.size == 0:
            return 1e100, np.zeros_like(uu, dtype=np.float64)
        cmax = float(c.max())
        if not math.isfinite(cmax) or cmax < 0.0:
            return 1e100, np.zeros_like(uu, dtype=np.float64)
        if T <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(T):
            T = max(1e-300, 1e-4 * cmax)
        scaled = (c - cmax) / T
        exps = np.exp(scaled, dtype=np.float64)
        sum_exp = float(exps.sum())
        if not math.isfinite(sum_exp) or sum_exp <= 0.0:
            w = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
            w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
            f = cmax
        else:
            f = cmax + T * float(np.log(sum_exp / float(c.size)))
            if not math.isfinite(f):
                f = cmax
            w = exps / sum_exp
        q = xfast(pp, w)
        gdp = 2.0 * q
        tdp = float(np.dot(pp, gdp))
        grad = pp * (gdp - tdp)
        grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)
        return float(f), grad

    def run_chunk(uu, T, end_stage):
        try:
            f0, _ = lse_func(uu, T)
        except Exception:
            return uu
        if not math.isfinite(f0):
            return uu
        best_u = np.array(uu, dtype=np.float64, copy=True)
        best_f = float(f0)
        stale = 0

        while time.monotonic() < end_stage - 2.0:
            maxit = 22 if n > 2200 else 36
            opts = {
                "maxiter": maxit,
                "gtol": 1e-10 if n > 1200 else 1e-11,
                "ftol": 1e-15,
                "maxls": 20,
                "maxcor": 20,
                "maxdisp": 0,
                "disp": False,
            }
            try:
                res = minimize(
                    lse_func,
                    best_u,
                    args=(T,),
                    method="L-BFGS-B",
                    jac=True,
                    options=opts,
                )
            except Exception:
                break

            if res.x is not None:
                x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
                if x.size == uu.size and np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
                    try:
                        fval = float(res.fun)
                    except Exception:
                        fval = math.inf
                    if math.isfinite(fval) and fval < best_f:
                        best_f = fval
                        best_u = x.copy()
                        stale = 0
                    else:
                        stale += 1
                        if stale >= 2:
                            break
            if getattr(res, "status", 1) == 0:
                break
        return best_u

    if fine_only:
        stages = ((0.003, 0.30), (0.0008, 0.45), (0.0002, 0.25))
    else:
        stages = (
            (0.20, 0.10),
            (0.06, 0.15),
            (0.018, 0.20),
            (0.005, 0.20),
            (0.0015, 0.25),
            (0.0004, 0.10),
        )

    start = time.monotonic()
    avail = float(stop_time - start - 12.0)
    if avail <= 5.0:
        return p

    best_p = p.copy()
    best_sc = _score_fft(p)
    total_w = float(sum(w for _, w in stages))
    if total_w <= 0.0:
        total_w = 1.0

    cum = 0.0
    for frac, wt in stages:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now > stop_time - 8.0:
            break
        cum += float(wt)
        end_stage = min(float(stop_time) - 6.0, start + avail * (cum / total_w))
        if end_stage <= now + 3.0:
            continue
        c = cfast(p)
        cmax = float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
        if not math.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 0.0:
            break
        T = max(1e-300, frac * cmax)
        u = run_chunk(u, T, end_stage)
        p_cur = _softmax(u)
        p_cur = np.maximum(p_cur, 0.0)
        ss = float(p_cur.sum())
        if ss > 1e-300:
            p_cur = p_cur / ss
        sc_cur = _score_fft(p_cur)
        if not math.isfinite(sc_cur):
            p = best_p.copy()
            u = p_to_u(p)
            continue
        if sc_cur < best_sc:
            best_p = p_cur.copy()
            best_sc = sc_cur
            p = p_cur.copy()
        elif sc_cur > best_sc + max(0.002, 2e-4 * best_sc):
            p = best_p.copy()
        else:
            p = p_cur.copy()
        u = p_to_u(p)
        if consider is not None:
            try:
                consider(best_p)
            except Exception:
                pass

    return best_p


def _coord_polish(p, stop_time, max_sweeps=100000, G=None, seed=12345, consider=None):
    n = int(np.asarray(p).size)
    p = np.maximum(np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel(), 0.0).copy()
    if n == 0:
        return p, math.inf
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s < 1e-300 or not math.isfinite(s):
        return p, math.inf
    p = p / s

    if G is None:
        if n <= 450:
            G = 56
        elif n <= 900:
            G = 44
        elif n <= 1800:
            G = 34
        elif n <= 3600:
            G = 26
        else:
            G = 20
    G = int(max(16, G))

    Ltot = 2 * n - 1
    c = _conv_self(p)
    S_total = 1.0
    cur_score = float(2.0 * n * c.max())
    best_score = cur_score
    best_p = p.copy()
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    sweep = 0
    flat = 0

    while sweep < max_sweeps and time.monotonic() < stop_time - 2.0:
        improved = False
        order = rng.permutation(n)
        for idx_i in order:
            i = int(idx_i)
            if time.monotonic() > stop_time - 0.6:
                break
            if S_total <= 1e-300:
                break
            if S_total > 1.25 or S_total < 0.75:
                inv = 1.0 / S_total
                p *= inv
                c *= inv * inv
                S_total = 1.0
            S = float(S_total)
            v = float(p[i])
            start = i
            end = min(i + n, Ltot)
            Ls = end - start
            if Ls <= 0:
                continue
            base = c[start:end]
            infl = 2.0 * p[:Ls]

            max_const = 0.0
            if start > 0:
                ml = float(c[:start].max()) if start > 0 else 0.0
                if ml > max_const:
                    max_const = ml
            if end < Ltot:
                mr = float(c[end:].max()) if end < Ltot else 0.0
                if mr > max_const:
                    max_const = mr

            x_hi = max(20.0 * v, 60.0 * S / float(n), 0.05 * S, 1e-12)
            x_hi = min(x_hi, 10.0 * S)
            if not math.isfinite(x_hi) or x_hi <= 0.0:
                x_hi = max(1e-8, S / float(n))

            pts = np.linspace(0.0, x_hi, G + 1, dtype=np.float64)
            dots = float(v)
            if 0.0 <= dots <= x_hi:
                pts = np.concatenate((pts, [dots]))
            pts = np.unique(pts)

            diag = i  # since start = i

            def eval_xs(xs, base=base, infl=infl, max_const=max_const, diag=diag):
                xs = np.asarray(xs, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
                w = xs - v
                denom = S + w
                vals = np.full(xs.shape, math.inf, dtype=np.float64)
                valid = denom > 1e-15
                if not np.any(valid):
                    return vals
                aff = base[None, :] + w[:, None] * infl[None, :]
                maff = np.max(aff, axis=1)
                dval = base[diag] + w * infl[diag] + w * w
                maff = np.maximum(maff, dval)
                maff = np.maximum(maff, max_const)
                dv = denom[valid]
                mv = maff[valid]
                vals[valid] = (2.0 * n * mv) / (dv * dv)
                return vals

            vals = eval_xs(pts)
            if not np.any(np.isfinite(vals)):
                continue
            imin = int(np.argmin(vals))
            best_x = float(pts[imin])
            best_val = float(vals[imin])

            if G > 1:
                dw = x_hi / float(G)
                lo = max(0.0, best_x - 2.0 * dw)
                hi = min(x_hi, best_x + 2.0 * dw)
                if hi <= lo:
                    hi = lo + 1e-12
                fine = np.linspace(lo, hi, 32, dtype=np.float64)
                if 0.0 <= best_x <= x_hi:
                    fine = np.concatenate((fine, [best_x]))
                fine = np.unique(fine)
                vals2 = eval_xs(fine)
                if np.any(np.isfinite(vals2)):
                    imin2 = int(np.argmin(vals2))
                    vx2 = float(fine[imin2])
                    vv2 = float(vals2[imin2])
                    if vv2 < best_val:
                        best_x = vx2
                        best_val = vv2

            if math.isfinite(best_val) and best_val < cur_score - 1e-12 and 0.0 <= best_x <= 10.0 * S:
                w = best_x - v
                if abs(w) > 1e-15:
                    p[i] = best_x
                    S_total += w
                    c[start:end] += w * infl
                    di = 2 * i
                    if 0 <= di < Ltot:
                        c[di] += w * w
                    cur_score = best_val
                    improved = True

        S = float(S_total)
        if S > 1e-300 and math.isfinite(S):
            inv = 1.0 / S
            p *= inv
            c *= inv * inv
        else:
            break
        S_total = 1.0
        c = _conv_self(p)
        np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
        new_score = float(2.0 * n * c.max())
        if math.isfinite(new_score) and new_score < best_score - 1e-15:
            best_score = new_score
            best_p = p.copy()
        if consider is not None:
            try:
                consider(best_p)
            except Exception:
                pass

        if improved:
            flat = 0
        else:
            flat += 1
            if flat >= 3:
                if time.monotonic() < stop_time - 10.0:
                    try:
                        p = p * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.035, n, dtype=np.float64))
                        p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
                        ss = float(p.sum())
                        if ss > 0.0:
                            p /= ss
                        c = _conv_self(p)
                        S_total = 1.0
                        cur_score = float(2.0 * n * c.max())
                        if math.isfinite(cur_score) and cur_score < best_score - 1e-14:
                            best_score = cur_score
                            best_p = p.copy()
                            if consider is not None:
                                consider(best_p)
                        flat = 0
                    except Exception:
                        break
                else:
                    break
        sweep += 1

    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    ss = float(p.sum())
    if ss > 1e-300:
        p = p / ss
    try:
        c = _conv_self(p)
        sc = float(2.0 * n * c.max())
    except Exception:
        sc = math.inf
    if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
        best_score = sc
        best_p = p.copy()
    return best_p, best_score


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(10.0, float(budget_s))
    if budget > 300.0:
        hard = t0 + budget - 25.0
    else:
        hard = t0 + budget - max(5.0, 0.1 * budget)
    if hard <= t0 + 2.0:
        hard = t0 + max(2.0, budget - 1.0)
    usable = max(1.0, hard - t0)
    final_reserve = max(10.0, 0.10 * usable) if usable > 80.0 else 0.0
    phase_limit = hard - final_reserve

    parent = _get_parent()
    best_arr = parent.copy()
    try:
        best_score = float(_score_exact_arr(best_arr))
    except Exception:
        best_score = math.inf

    if not math.isfinite(best_score) or best_arr.size == 0:
        best_arr = np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64)
        best_arr /= 64.0
        try:
            best_score = float(_score_exact_arr(best_arr))
        except Exception:
            best_score = math.inf

    def consider(arr):
        nonlocal best_arr, best_score
        try:
            a = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        except Exception:
            return
        a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
        a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if a.size == 0:
            return
        s = float(a.sum())
        if s < 0.01:
            return
        try:
            sc = float(_score_exact_arr(a))
        except Exception:
            return
        if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-13:
            a = a / s
            best_arr = a.copy()
            best_score = sc

    def make_skew(src, n, sd):
        p = _resample_mid(src, n)
        rng = np.random.default_rng(sd)
        x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
        side = 1.0 if rng.random() < 0.5 else -1.0
        amp = 0.5 + 0.9 * float(rng.random())
        q = p * np.exp(side * amp * (2.0 * x - 1.0))
        q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
        s = float(q.sum())
        if s > 0.0:
            q /= s
        else:
            q = p
        return q

    def run_attempt(n, src_arr, end_time, sd, do_coord=True):
        nonlocal best_arr, best_score
        n = int(max(1, n))
        p = _resample_mid(src_arr, n)
        if p.size != n or not np.all(np.isfinite(p)) or p.sum() <= 0.0:
            p = np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
        p = p / float(p.sum())
        consider(p)

        p_start = _score_fft(p)
        if best_arr.size == n:
            b_start = _score_fft(best_arr)
            if math.isfinite(b_start) and p_start > b_start + 0.02:
                # This initialization is clearly losing to the saved same-length point.
                return

        if time.monotonic() > end_time - 12.0:
            return
        phase_len = max(0.0, float(end_time) - time.monotonic())

        lbfgs_stop = min(float(end_time) - 12.0, time.monotonic() + 0.72 * phase_len)
        if lbfgs_stop > time.monotonic() + 12.0:
            try:
                p2 = _lbfgs_optimize(p, n, lbfgs_stop, consider=consider, fine_only=False)
                if p2 is not None and p2.size == n and np.all(np.isfinite(p2)) and p2.sum() > 0.0:
                    p = p2 / float(p2.sum())
            except Exception:
                pass
            if best_arr.size == n:
                if math.isfinite(_score_fft(best_arr)) and _score_fft(best_arr) < _score_fft(p) - 0.002:
                    p = best_arr.copy()

        rem = float(end_time) - time.monotonic()
        if do_coord and rem > 20.0:
            coord_stop = min(
                float(end_time) - 2.0,
                time.monotonic() + max(20.0, min(160.0, 0.28 * phase_len)),
            )
            try:
                p3, _ = _coord_polish(p, coord_stop, max_sweeps=100000, seed=sd, consider=consider)
                if p3 is not None and p3.size == n and np.all(np.isfinite(p3)) and p3.sum() > 0.0:
                    p = p3
            except Exception:
                pass
            if best_arr.size == n:
                if math.isfinite(_score_fft(best_arr)) and _score_fft(best_arr) < _score_fft(p) - 0.002:
                    p = best_arr.copy()

        if p is not None and p.size == n and np.all(np.isfinite(p)) and float(p.sum()) > 0.0:
            consider(p)

    # Build a small set of lengths, prioritizing the available parent length.
    parent_len = int(best_arr.size) if best_arr.size > 0 else 384
    lengths = []
    cur = parent_len
    max_len = 3072
    while cur <= max_len:
        lengths.append(cur)
        if cur * 2 > max_len:
            break
        cur *= 2
    if (not lengths) or lengths[-1] < 3072:
        for cand in (1024, 2048, 3072):
            if cand > lengths[-1] if lengths else True:
                lengths.append(cand)
        lengths = sorted(set(lengths))
        if len(lengths) > 5:
            lengths = lengths[-5:]
    if not lengths:
        lengths = [384]

    k = len(lengths)
    if k == 1:
        weights = [1.0]
    elif k == 2:
        weights = [0.45, 0.55]
    elif k == 3:
        weights = [0.28, 0.34, 0.38]
    elif k == 4:
        weights = [0.18, 0.25, 0.30, 0.27]
    else:
        base = 0.85 / float(k)
        weights = [base] * k
        weights[-1] += 1.0 - sum(weights)
    sw = float(sum(weights))
    if sw > 0:
        weights = [w / sw for w in weights]

    phase_limit = min(phase_limit, hard - 15.0)
    rng_top = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    for idx, L in enumerate(lengths):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= phase_limit - 8.0:
            break
        rem_to_end = max(0.0, phase_limit - now)
        if rem_to_end <= 15.0:
            break
        w_rem = float(sum(weights[idx:]))
        if w_rem <= 0:
            w_rem = 1.0
        end_phase = min(phase_limit, now + (weights[idx] / w_rem) * rem_to_end)
        if end_phase <= now + 12.0:
            continue

        src = best_arr.copy() if best_arr.size > 0 else parent.copy()

        # First length gets one deliberately asymmetric second start, if affordable.
        if idx == 0 and (end_phase - now) > 180.0:
            v0 = src
            v1 = make_skew(src, L, int(seed + 1234561))
            mid = min(end_phase, now + 0.55 * (end_phase - now))
            run_attempt(L, v0, mid, int(seed + 100), do_coord=True)
            if time.monotonic() < end_phase - 15.0:
                run_attempt(L, v1, end_phase, int(seed + 200), do_coord=True)
        else:
            run_attempt(L, src, end_phase, int(seed + 500 + L + int(rng_top.integers(0, 10000))), do_coord=True)

    # Final squeeze: refined LSE plus coordinate polish on the saved exact best.
    final_end = min(hard - 10.0, t0 + budget - 6.0)
    if final_end > time.monotonic() + 45.0 and best_arr.size > 0:
        nbest = int(best_arr.size)
        rem = final_end - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 130.0 and nbest <= 3500:
            lstop = min(final_end - 55.0, time.monotonic() + 0.42 * rem)
            try:
                p2 = _lbfgs_optimize(best_arr, nbest, lstop, consider=consider, fine_only=True)
                if p2 is not None and p2.size == nbest and np.all(np.isfinite(p2)) and p2.sum() > 0.0:
                    p2 = p2 / float(p2.sum())
                    consider(p2)
            except Exception:
                p2 = best_arr.copy()
            else:
                p2 = best_arr.copy() if best_arr.size == nbest and _score_fft(best_arr) < _score_fft(p2) - 1e-10 else p2
        else:
            p2 = best_arr.copy()

        cstop = min(final_end - 8.0, time.monotonic() + max(30.0, 0.35 * (final_end - time.monotonic())))
        if cstop > time.monotonic() + 15.0:
            try:
                p3, _ = _coord_polish(p2, cstop, max_sweeps=100000, seed=int(seed + 987654321), consider=consider)
                if p3 is not None and p3.size == nbest and np.all(np.isfinite(p3)) and p3.sum() > 0.0:
                    p3 = p3 / float(p3.sum())
                    consider(p3)
            except Exception:
                pass

    # Final validation and return.
    try:
        out = np.asarray(best_arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        out = np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)
        out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if out.size == 0 or float(out.sum()) < 0.01:
            out = parent.copy()
            out = np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)
            out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if out.size == 0 or float(out.sum()) < 0.01:
                out = np.full(64, 1.0 / 64.0, dtype=np.float64)
        ls = [float(x) for x in out]
        final_sc = float(evaluate_sequence(ls))
        if not math.isfinite(final_sc):
            ls = [float(x) for x in parent]
            final_sc = float(evaluate_sequence(ls))
            if not math.isfinite(final_sc):
                return [1.0]
        return ls
    except Exception:
        return [1.0]
```
